Day Two of the *Magic Online* Championship began with a Booster Draft using two packs of *Aether Revolt* and one pack of *Kaladesh*, in that order.

The decks that the sixteen competitors built can be found below. If you'd like to see how they ended up with those card pools, please refer to their Draft Viewers.

[Draft Viewer – Pod 1](https://web.archive.org/web/20170304000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=3_4_2017_1)

[Draft Viewer – Pod 2](https://web.archive.org/web/20170304000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=3_4_2017_2)

#### Saturday Draft - Anssi Alkio
